The fourth division is the realm of the firmaments and all the lowly material world. (This division] is called “Making.” It consists of ten firmaments. The light of the sefirot spreads among them, and they are called the ten sefirot of Making, which consists of the spreading of holiness in the midst of the material matter in which the “outside” ones rule.

footnotes:
³ The reference is to the “Palaces” (Heikhalot


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:4)


ואלו נקראות, אצילות בריאה יצירה עשיה וסימנם אבי״ע. והענין כי האצילות יתעלה על בריאה, והבריאה על היצירה, והיצירה על העשיה. וארבע מחלוקות אלו ימצאו באצילות ובבריאה וביצירה ובעשיה, מפני היות המדרגות יורדות מעלה לעלול:


[Thus] these [four divisions] are called Emanation, Creation, Formation, and Making. Their acronym is ABYA. Thus Emanation is above Creation, and Creation is above Formation, and Formation is above Making. These four divisions may be found in Emanation, and in Creation, and in Formation, and in Making, since the degrees descend from cause to caused.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:5)


(שער י״ז), ל״ט, עוד צריך לדעת כי הנצח מששת ימי בראשית היתה יניקתו מצד הגבורה וההוד מצד החסד, בסוד המזגת המדות. ומאז גרם החטא ונתבלבלו הצנורות לא מצאו כל אנשי חיל ידיהם לתקן הפגם, עד שהצדיק יעקב אבינו את הדין על עצמו באמרו, "אם יבא עשו" – למטה ושרו למעלה לעורר דין, "אל המחנה האחת" – הוא ההוד נקבה שר צבאות, מחנה וצבא חד הוא, ומעתה לא מיבעיא שתש כח ההוד כנקבה, אלא "והכהו" – שהחליש כח הקומה בכללה הרמוזה בוא״ו שבשם אמת ליעקב ושחת רחמיו, "והיה המחנה הנשאר" – הוא הנצח מתחזק לפליטה לאחוז במדת החסד. וכן היה, כי נגע שרו של עשו בכף ירך יעקב ומיד נתהפך סדר היניקה, ושב ההוד מושפע מצד הגבורה, בסוד "והודי נהפך עלי למשחית". והנצח היה רופף, עד שבא דוד המלך ע״ה ותקנו בזמריו להיות יונק מצד החסד, וזהו סוד "נעימות בימינך נצח".


[Sec. 17] 39. [The beginner] must also know that the [sefirah] Neẓaḥ, during the six days of creation, derived sustenance from the side of Gevurah,⁴ and Hod from [the side of] Ḥesed, in the mystery of the mingling of the Qualities. Since because of [Adam’s] sin the [sefirotic] connections have been confused,⁵ none of the men of valor were able to repair this defect until the righteous one, Jacob our father, [took] Judgment upon himself, saying, *If Esau comes* below and his [angelic] “minister” above⁶ to arouse Judgment *on the one camp* (Gen. 32:9), [namely,] the female Hod. ([She is called] the captain of the hosts, [since] camp and hosts are the same. Thus the fact that Hod’s strength was weak like a woman[’s] is not difficult [to comprehend].) Now [we understand] *and strikes it* [in the sense that “Esau”] weakened the strength of the [sefirotic] stature in general. This is hinted at by [the additional letter] *vav* in the name [of Jacob in the verse] *truth unto Jacob* (Mic. 7:20). Thus he destroyed his mercy. *And the remaining camp,* which is Neẓaḥ, strengthens itself as a remnant to grasp on to the quality of Ḥesed. Thus it was that the minister of Esau touched the thigh of Jacob and immediately the order of nourishment was changed and Hod returned, influenced from the side of Gevurah⁷ in the mystery of *My comeliness was turned in me into corruption* (Dan. 10:8). Neẓaḥ was [thus] weakened until King David, peace upon him, came and repaired it with his melodies⁸ so that it might derive nourishment from the side of Ḥesed. This is the mystery of [the verse] *in Your right hand is bliss forevermore* (*neẓaḥ*) (Ps. 16:11).

footnotes:
⁴ In the “normative” sefirotic order, Neẓaḥ is found on the “side” of Gedulah (Ḥesed), while Hod is on the side of Gevurah.
⁵ This is an expression of the idea that sin on the earthly plane causes “disruption” in the order of the sefirot on high.
⁶ Cf. Rashi on Gen. 32:25.
⁷ The “touching” apparently restored the sefirotic state of “confusion.” Cf. n. 4 above.
⁸ The psalms.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:6)


ומה מתוק מדבש עם ההקדמה הזאת מה שנמצא בקצת מדרשים שהיה מיכאל שר החסד הנוגע בכף ירך יעקב, כי הא והא איתא, אחרי שהחסד הסכים לדחות מדת ההוד מאצלו שלא יתמזג דינה. וכיון שהחסד נאחז יפה יפה ונקשר בימינא, הוד גם כן נקשר בשמאלא, להוכיח במישור מלכי יהודה וכל עם הארץ לכלה הפשע ולהתם חטאת, ולפי שעה בעת רצון זכה דוד המלך ע״ה ונעשה רצונו למזג המדות כימי עולם, בסוד "כהניך ילבשו צדק וחסידיך ירננו" המבואר בזוהר (זהר ח״א דף קמח.), וכן עתיד להתחדש ולהתמיד לימות המשיח:


After this introduction, how much sweeter than honey is [the passage] found in some midrashim that Michael, the angel of Mercy, was the one to touch the thigh of Jacob,⁹ for [both] this and that come after Ḥesed agreed to push away the quality of Hod from beside it so that its [characteristic of] Judgment would not commingle. Then, when Ḥesed was grasped very well and it was tied up on the right [side],¹⁰ Hod also was tied to the left [side] in order to reprove with equity the kings of Judah and all the people of the land so as to put an end to sin and to stop transgression. At that time, which was one of [divine] will, King David, peace upon him, merited that His will be done to arrange the [sefirotic] qualities as in olden days in the mystery of *Your priests will garb themselves with righteousness, and Your pious ones will rejoice* (Ps. 132:9), which is explained in the Zohar.¹¹ Thus it is destined to renew itself permanently in the days of the Messiah.

footnotes:
⁹ Cf. Bereshit Rabbah 78:1. In the Zohar, Michael symbolizes the sefirah Gedulah/Ḥesed (I, 98b-99a).
¹⁰ I.e., its “proper” side.
¹¹ Zohar I, 148a.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:7)


(שער י״ח), מ׳‎, עוד צריך לדעת כי המלכות והת״ת אצילותם מרום המעלות יחד. ותחלתם מחכמה, וזהו סוד, "דו פרצופין נבראו", אלא שבחכמה הם ו׳‎ על ד׳‎ כסדר מילואה של יו״ד שבשם. ומתאצלים ובאים בבינה ד׳‎ על ו׳‎, ובבינה על ידי חסד נפרדת המלכות מהתפארת, ונשארת המלכות בחכמה שבבינה, ומתאצל התפארת. ואחר כך ויבן ה׳‎ אלהים – בינה, את הצלע – מלכות, אשר לקח מן האדם – חכמה, ויביאה אל האדם – תפארת. ועדיין אין זה סוד המעוט אבל היתה המלכות למטה מהתפארת, שניהם יונקים הדין מן הגבורה מהבינה, וכן החסד מן הגדולה מהבינה, והתפארת מכרעת המלכות אל צד החסד שכן הוא מטה כלפי חסד, ואז היא קטרגה ואמרה אי אפשר כו׳‎, מפני שהיתה כונתה שתהיה המלכות פועלת הדין ות״ת פועלת החסד, ולא יכריע התפארת את המלכות לצד החסד, ואז נאמר לה לכי ומעטי את עצמך וגרעו אורה, ומה שהיתה במדרגה שביעית ירדה במדרגה עשירית, מפני מעוט זכותה:


[Sec. 18] 40. [The beginner] must also know that the emanation of Malkhut and Tiferet in union stems from the height of the degrees beginning from Ḥokhmah. This is the mystery of the dual face¹² in which they were created. However, within Ḥokhmah they constitute the *vav* prior to the *daled* in the order of the completion of the [spelling of] *yod* in the [divine] Name.¹³ is spelled out with three letters: *yod, vav,* and *daled*. They emanate and come in to Binah as *daled* before *vav*. In Binah, through Ḥesed, Malkhut is separated from Tiferet and Malkhut remains in Ḥokhmah-within-Binah. Tiferet emanates, and afterwards *And the Lord built* (*banah*), Binah, *the rib,* Malkhut, *which he took from the man,* Ḥokhmah, *and brought it to the man,* Tiferet (Gen. 2:22). *And there was still not [a help meet for him]* (Gen. 2:20)—this is the mystery of diminution.¹⁴ However, Malkhut was below Tiferet, with both of them deriving sustenance from [the side of] Judgment from Gevurah and Binah. Similarly, Ḥesed [derived its sustenance] from Gedulah and Binah. Tiferet forced Malkhut to the side of Mercy, for he is “below” relative to Mercy. Then [Malkhut] criticized and said, “It is impossible,” since her intention was that Malkhut should work [in the sphere of] Judgment and Tiferet in Mercy, and that Tiferet would not force Malkhut to the side of Mercy. Then we say to her, “Go and diminish yourself.” Thus they subtracted from its light, and whereas previously she had been at the seventh rung, she descended to the tenth [rung] because of the diminution of her merit.

footnotes:
¹² I.e. a hermaphrodite. Cf. Eruvin 18a, Bereshit Rabbah 8:1.
¹³ The word yod
¹⁴ The reference is to the diminution of the moon. Cf. Bereshit Rabbah 6:3. The moon is a symbol of the sefirah Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:8)


(שער י״ט), מ״א, עוד צריך לדעת כי עם היות השמות שאינם נמחקים רבים והם עשר שמות עשר ספירות, יש שם אחד הכולל עשר ספירות והוא שם בן ארבע יהו״ה. והכונה, י׳‎ בחכמה, ה׳‎ בבינה, ו׳‎ כולל שית ספירן, ה׳‎ בתראה מלכות. ואין סוף נעלם בכתר, ואתפשט נהוריה בארבע אתוון אלין, מיו״ד לה״א, ומה״א לוא״ו, ומוא״ו לה״א אחרונה. ואולם שם זה יתמלא בעשר אותיות לרמוז לזה והם, יו״ד ה״א וא״ו ה״א. ונקודות השם גם כן יורה על עשר ספירות כזה, יהו״ה חולם כתר, על קמץ תפארת, שלש מכאן הם חכמה בינה חסד, ושלש מכאן הם נצח הוד יסוד, נקודת קמץ בגבורה, האותיות עצמם מלכות. ויתמלא לרמוז לסוד עשר גם ביודי״ן כזה, יו״ד ה״י וי״ו ה״י, ושם זה בחכמה שבכתר, ושם מלא אלפי״ן בחכמה ממש: ואולם אחר שהספירות כל אחת כלולה מעשר גם שם בן ארבע נמצא בכל אחת מהן והוא, יהו״ה כלו קמוץ – בכתר, יהו״ה כלו פתח – בחכמה, יהו״ה כלו בצירי – בבינה, יהו״ה כלו בסגול – בחסד, יהו״ה כלו בשבא – בגבורה, יהו״ה כלו בחולם – בתפארת, יהו״ה כלו בחירק – בנצח, יהו״ה כלו בשורק – בהוד, יהו״ה כלו בשורק – ביסוד, המלכות בשם אותיות פשוטות מפני שהיא כלולה מכלם ופועלת כפי השפעת כל ספירה וספירה עליה, ולפיכך נקודתה כפי יניקתה:


[Sec. 19] 41. [The beginner] must also know that while the [divine] names which may not be erased¹⁵ are numerous, and while there are ten names [corresponding to] the ten sefirot, there exists one general name for all the sefirot. It is the four-letter name YDVD. The meaning is that Y = Ḥokhmah, H = Binah, V includes six sefirot,¹⁶ is six. The six sefirot are those from Gedulah to Hod. and the final H = Malkhut. Eyn Sof disappears into Keter, and its light spreads in these four letters from *yod* to *heh,* and from *heh* to *vav,* and from *vav* to the final *heh*. However, this name will become full with ten letters to hint at this. These [letters are] *yod, heh, vav, heh*.¹⁷ The punctuation of the Name also indicates the ten sefirot in this manner: YODEVADE. The *ḥolem*¹⁸ [indicates] Keter, the *kamaẓ*¹⁹ sound. [indicates] Tiferet, the first [*segol*]²⁰ sound. [indicates] Ḥokhmah, Binah, Ḥesed, and the last [*segol*] indicates Neẓaḥ, Hod, Yesod. The dot in the *kamaḥ* [hints] at Gevurah, and the letters themselves are Malkhut. [Thus the Name is] expanded to hint at the mystery of Ten. [This is] also [true concerning] the *yods* in this manner: YOD HY VYV HY. This name is [contained] in Ḥokhmah-within-Keter. The expanded name with *‘alef*s is within the actual Ḥokhmah. However, since each of the sefirot contains the ten [sefirot], the four[-lettered] Name is found in each of them. Thus YAHAVAHA, [voweled] entirely with *kamaẓ* is in Keter, YADAVADA, [voweled] entirely with *pataḥ,*²¹ sound. is in Ḥokhmah, YEDEVEDE, [voweled] entirely with *ẓere,*²² sound. is in Binah, YEDEVEDE, [voweled] entirely with *segol,* is in Ḥesed. YIDIVIDI, [voweled] entirely with *shva,*²³ or else no sound. is in Gevurah, YODOVODO, [voweled] entirely with *ḥolem,*²⁴. is in Tiferet YIDIVIDI, [voweled] entirely with *ḥirik,*²⁵ sound. is in Neẓaḥ, YUDUVUDU, [voweled] entirely with *shuruk,*²⁶ sound. is in Hod, YUDUD, [voweled] entirely in *shuruk,* is in Yesod. Malkhut [goes] with the Name of the plain [unvoweled] letters since she is made up of all of the [sefirot] and acts according to the influence of each and every sefirah upon it. Therefore she is voweled according to her [source of] sustenance.

footnotes:
¹⁵ Cf. Sifrei on Deuteronomy, piska 61, on Deut. 12:4. Cf. also Shevuot 35a.
¹⁶ The numerical value of vav
¹⁷ The letters are spelled out in full, thus equaling ten. Cf. n. 13 above.
¹⁸ Hebrew vowel with the o sound.
¹⁹ Hebrew vowel with the a
²⁰ Hebrew vowel with the e
²¹ Hebrew vowel with the a
²² Hebrew vowel with the e
²³ Hebrew vowel signifying either a short e
²⁴ I.e., o
²⁵ Hebrew vowel with the e
²⁶ Hebrew vowel with the u


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:9)


(שער כ׳‎), מ״ב, יש היכלות ולבוש אל השמות האלו והם עשר שמות שאינם נמחקין. שם אל הכתר – אהי״ה, וזה מורה העלם אל הכתר ועתיד להגלות ולא נגלה כלל, ולכך שם זה נהגה באותיוו מפני שהגיונו מורה העלמו. ושם זה אל הכתר מצד הבינה שבו, ומצד חכמה שבו שמו שם בן ארבע, ומצד עצמו אין לו שם ונקודה:


[Sec. 20] 42. There exist “palaces” and “garments” for these Names. They are the ten [divine] Names which are not to be erased. The Name for Keter is Ehyeh.²⁷ This [Name] indicates the sublimity of Keter, which is to be revealed in the future but which has not [yet] been revealed at all.²⁸ Therefore this Name is pronounced according to its letters, since its pronunciation teaches its sublimity. This Name appertains to Keter from the aspect of Binah [inherent] within it. From the aspect of Ḥokhmah [inherent] within it, its name is the four[-letter] Name. It itself has neither name nor vowel.

footnotes:
²⁷ Cf. Exod. 3:14.
²⁸ Cf. Zohar III, 152a, in which the righteous, in time to come, will be destined to look upon the “soul of the soul” of the Torah, which is identified with Keter.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:10)


ואל החכמה – י״ה, ושם זה מורה על שתוף חכמה ובינה יחד. ושם הבינה – יהו״ה מורה על השפעת הדין והרחמים מן הבינה, וגם יורה על שנאצל ממנה התפארת הנקראת יהו״ה, והמלכות הנקראת אלהים: ושם החסד – א״ל, ושם זה יורה על התפשטות החסד מחכמה, וגם יורה בצורת האלף כלולות שלשה קוים בחסד, דהיינו חסד וגבורה בשני יודי״ן, ותפארת ו׳‎ שעורה דתרווייהו באמצע, וגם יורה היותה כוללת היחוד וגורמת אליו, כי הוי״ו תפארת, ומלכות ויסוד נכללים ביו״ד תתאה, ושלש ראשונות מאירים שם ביו״ד עילאה, ול׳‎ יורה על הבינה השופעת להדיא על הכל:


Ḥokhmah’s [Name] is Yah. This Name indicates the cooperation of Ḥokhmah and Binah together. The name of Binah is YIHEVID, which indicates the influence of Judgment and Mercy from Binah and also indicates [the fact that] Tiferet, which is called YDVD, emanated from her. Malkhut is called Elokim. The Name for Ḥesed is El. This Name indicates the spreading of Ḥesed from Ḥokhmah and also indicates in the form of the *‘alef* the completion of the three branches in Ḥesed, which are Ḥesed and Gevurah in the two *yods,* and Tiferet [in the] *vav*.²⁹ (N) is thus seen as a combination of two *yods* and a *vav*. The measure of both of them is in the center. It also indicates that it encompasses the unity and causes it, for the *vav* [indicates] Tiferet and Malkhut and Yesod, [which] are included in the lower *yod*. The three first [sefirot] shine there in the upper *yod. Lamed* indicates Binah, which influences directly over all.

footnotes:
²⁹ Alef


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:11)


ושם הגבורה – אלהים, ושם זה יורה על בינה הנקראת מ״י, עם שלשה אבות הנקראים אל״ה. וגם שם זה ימצא בגבורה ובבינה ובמלכות והכל רומז אל שלשה אלה מקומות הדין:


The [divine] Name for Gevurah is ‘Elohim. This name indicates Binah, which is called “Who,” [along] with the three Patriarchs,³⁰ has created *these*.” The implication is that Binah begot the three sefirotic “patriarchs.” who are called “Those” (eleh). This Name may be found in Gevurah and in Binah and in Malkhut. It is all a hint at “those” three, [which are] places of Judgment.

footnotes:
³⁰ The three sefirot, Gedulah, Gevurah, and Tiferet, are identified with the three patriarchs, Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob. Cf. Isa. 40:26: “See who


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:12)


ושם התפארת – יהו״ה, מורה רק באבותיך חש״ק, חול״ם שב״א קמ״ץ, וארבע אותיות יורה, על תרין עטרין דירית מאבא ואימא, דהיינו י׳‎ חכמה, ה׳‎ בינה, ושתיהם נכללות בוא״ו, והיא משפעת במלכות:


The [divine] Name of Tiferet is YIDAVD, which is indicated [by the verse] *Only the Lord had a delight (ḥashak) in your fathers* (Deut. 10:15). *Ḥashak* [is an acronym for the vowels] *ḥolem, shva, kamaẓ* The four letters indicate the two crowns which succeed Father and Mother, i.e., *Yod* - Ḥokhmah, *heh* = Binah. Both of them are included in *vav,* which influences Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:13)


ושם הנצח וההוד – צבאות, מורה שהם ממשיכים כח הזכר על ידי היסוד אל הנקבה. ואל היסוד – שד״י, מורה על היותו הספיק השפע אל הנקבה, וגם שעד היסוד הגיע האצילות וכאן אמר לעולמו די, כי המלכות למעלה ממנו כדפירשנו. ויש שיחסו לו שם א״ל ח״י ונתבאר בספר פרדס.


The [divine] Name for Neẓaḥ and Hod is *Ẓeva’ot,* which teaches that they continue the strength of the Male through Yesod to the Female [Malkhut]. [The divine Name] for Yesod is Shaddai, which indicates its supplying of overflow to the Female. Also, the emanation continues up to Yesod. Here [God] said, “Enough [of emanation]!” to His world,³¹ for Malkhut is above it, as we have explained. There are those who have ascribed to it the name “Living God.” This is explained in the Book *Pardes [Rimmonim]*.

footnotes:
³¹ Cf. the etymology of Shaddai in Ḥagigah 12a: “I [God] am the one who said to the world, ‘Enough!’”


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/5:14)


ואל המלכות שם – אדנ״י, מורה ענינים רבים ונתבארו שם. וארבע אותיות אלו לבוש אל ארבע אותיות השם בן ארבע כזה יאהדונה״י ובזה נכלל הפרק הזה:


In Malkhut the [divine] Name Adonai indicates many matters which are explained [in *Pardes Rimmonim]*. These four letters are a “garment” for the four letters of the four-letter Name [which when combined looks] like this: YAHDVNHY. With this the chapter is completed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/6:1)


(שער כ״א), מ״ג, עוד צריך לדעת כי יש שמות קדושים תחת אלו והם כמו כנויים אליהם. ויחסו אל הכתר שם ארארית״א. ואל החכמה כ״ב אותיות בשם י״ה כזה, אי״ה בי״ה כו׳‎. וזו אל שלשה ראשונות יחד כוז״ו במוכס״ז כוז״ו. ואל החסד שם ע״ב, והוא יוצא מפסוקי ויס״ע ויב״א וי״ט, ובו רי״ו אותיות, כמנין ארי״ה שבחסד. ואל הגבורה שם מ״ב, והוא היוצא מבראשית עד וב׳‎ של ובהו, מ״ב אותיות, והם שבע שמות כנגד שבע ספירות.


[Sec. 21] 43. [The beginner] must also know that there are holy Names inferior to these. They are similar to appellations for them. [Thus] they have ascribed to Keter the Name Araryta, and to Ḥokhmah the twenty-two [Hebrew] letters with [the suffix] *yah,* like this: *ayah, byah,* etc. This [Name] designates the three first [sefirot] together: Khuzu Behokhsaz Khuzu. To Ḥesed belongs the seventy-two letter Name which comes out of the verses *He traveled … he came… he pitched* (Exod. 19:2). It contains 216 letters like the numerical [equivalent] of *aryeh* in Ḥesed. Gevurah has the forty-two letter Name which comes out of [the verses] from *In the beginning* (Gen. 1:1) to the *bet* in *bohu* (“void”), [equaling] forty-two letters. They constitute seven Names corresponding to the seven sefirot.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/6:2)


ואל התפארת שם של כ״ב היוצא מיברכך כו׳‎. ואל הנצח שם בן כ״ב אחר. ואל ההוד, שם היוצא מפסוק את ה׳‎ אלהיך תירא כו׳‎. ואל היסוד י״ב הויות של שם בן ארבע. ואל המלכות שם מטטרון. ובאור שמות אלו פירשתי בארוכה בספר פרדס:


Tiferet possesses the Name of twenty-two [letters] which comes out from *May the Lord bless you* (Num. 6:24). Neẓaḥ has another Name of twenty-two [letters]. Hod has a Name which stems from the verse *You will fear the Lord your God* (Deut. 6:13, 10:20). Yesod possesses twelve permutations of the four[-letter] Name. Malkhut has the Name Metatron.¹ pp. 377-381. I have explained the meaning of these Names at length in the book *Pardes [Rimmonim]*.

footnotes:
¹ On the angel Metatron, see Scholem, Kabbalah,


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/6:3)


ואל הספירות עוד כנויים שהם לבוש אל השמות שאינם נמחקים, והם כנויים אל פעולות הספירות ועניינם, ולא כדרך הסכמת החכמים ודרך מקרה כאשר חשבו רבים, אלא מתיחסים אל הספירות לפי עניינם. ויתרבו הכנויים במדה אחת מטעם שפעמים תינק המדה ממה שלמעלה מפאת הדין ואז לה שם אחד, ופעמים תינק מהרחמים ולה שם אחר. המשל בזה, חכמה לה שם אחד מצד עצמה, ושם שני מצד השפעתה בחסד, ושם שלישי מצד קבלתה מהכתר, וישתנו השמות כפי קבלתה מבחינת הכתר, ותשתנה עוד כפי צד השפעתה בחסד, וכן על דרך זה לפי השתנות הבחינות:


The sefirot also possess appellations which serve as a garment [concealing] the Names which are not to be erased. They are appellations of the actions of the sefirot and their relations. It is not, as many have thought, [that they are named] by agreement of the sages or by chance. [The Names] are, rather, ascribed to the sefirot according to their characteristics. Many appellations have been attached to one quality for the reason that sometimes it derives its sustenance from above from the side of Judgment, at which time one Name [is appropriate], and at other times it will derive sustenance from [the side of] Mercy and has another name. An example of this is that Ḥokhmah has one Name in its own right and a second Name due to its influence over Ḥesed and a third Name due to its receiving [emanation] from Keter. The Names differ according to its receipt from the aspect of Keter and differ further according to its influence over Ḥesed. Thus in this way [its Name changes] with the change of aspects.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/6:4)


(שער כ״ב), מ״ד, עוד צריך לדעת כי יש ביכולת הצדיקים להיותם מרכבה אל המדות בסוד המשכת נשמתם ופעולתם בעולם הזה, ונטייתם אל אחד מהצדדים ואחת המדות, כענין אברהם איש החסד, ויצחק איש הגבורה, ויעקב בעל התפארת, ומשה בעל הנצח, ואהרן בעל ההוד. ועוד לדרך אחר, אהרן איש החסד, משה איש הדעת הנעלם בתפארת, יוסף בעל היסוד, וכן פלטי בן ליש, וכן בועז, וכן שלמה, יחס לשלמה בסוד הדעת במציאותו הכלול ביסוד, ורבים כאלה, ובו דוד בעל המלכות, וכן רוב המלכים הצדיקים, וענין זה הארכנו בספר פרדס בס״ד:


[Sec. 22] 44. [The beginner] must also know that the righteous have the power to constitute a chariot for the Qualities in the mystery of the continuity of their souls and actions in this world and their orientation to one of the [divine] aspects and one of the qualities. Thus Abraham [was termed] a man of Ḥesed, Isaac a man of Gevurah, and Jacob the master of Tiferet; Moses was the master of Neẓaḥ, and Aaron the master of Hod. [There are] more [correspondences] in a different manner: Aaron [could be considered] a man of Ḥesed; Moses the man of Da’at, which is subumed within Tiferet² pp. 107 ff.; Joseph [was] master of Yesod, and thus [also] Palti ben Laish and Boaz and Solomon. Solomon relates to the mystery of Da’at in its general existence in Yesod. There are many similar [correspondences] in which David is the master of Malkhut and thus most of the righteous kings. We have dealt with this matter at length in the book *Pardes [Rimmonim],* with God’s help.

footnotes:
² On the position of Da’at within the sefirotic system, see Scholem, Kabbalah,


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/6:5)


ואולם האנשים המיוחדים אשר נקבו בשמות שהן מוסדי דור ודור בכללות קומת האצילות הם, אדם נח שם – חכמה בינה דעת. אברהם יצחק יעקב – חסד פחד תפארת. משה ואהרן פינחס ודוד – נצח הוד יסוד מלכות. וכלם בשרשי הנשמות אשר לאדם השלם באברי השכינה אם כל חי, דמתמן, מלכים, צדיקים, חוזים נביאים, אנשי אמת, גבורים, חסידים, נבונים, חכמים, ראשי אלפי ישראל:


However, these special men who are called by name, who are the foundations of every generation in the generality of the stature of emanation, are: Adam, Noah, Shem, Ḥokhmah, Binah, Da’at; Abraham, Isaac, Jacob, Ḥesed, Paḥad,³ Tiferet; Moses, Aaron, Pinḥas, and David, Neẓaḥ, Hod, Yesod, Malkhut. All of them [partake] of the roots of the souls which pertain to the complete Adam in the wings of the Shekhinah, *mother of all living* [cf. Gen. 3:20]. From this [source stem] kings, righteous men, seers, prophets, men of truth, heroes, pious men, understanding men, wise men, the heads of the thousands of Israel.

footnotes:
³ Cf. Gen. 31:42.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VI 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VI/r/6:6)


מ״ה, עוד צריך לדעת כי בתפארת י״ב גבולי אלכסון, והם גבול דרומית רומית, גבול דרומית תחתית, גבול דרומית מזרחית, גבול דרומית מערבית הם ארבעה. עוד גבול צפונית רומית, גבול צפונית תחתית, גבול צפונית מערבית, גבול צפונית מזרחית הם ארבעה. עוד גבול מזרחית רומית, גבול מזרחית תחתית, גבול מערבית רומית, גבול מערבית תחתית הם ארבעה, הם י״ב נגד י״ב אורות עליונות להם י״ב הויות, להם מרכבה י״ב שבטי ישראל. ובאור ענין זה בארוכה בספר פרדס בס״ד:


45. [The beginner] must also know that in Tiferet there are twelve diagonal borders. These are the upper southern border, the lower southern border, the southeastern border, the southwestern border, totaling four. Also the upper northern border, the lower northern border, the northwestern border, the northeastern border, totaling four. Also the upper eastern border, the lower eastern border, the upper western border, and the lower western border, totaling four. They constitute twelve, corresponding to the twelve upper lights. They possess twelve existences. To them the twelve tribes of Israel constitute a chariot. The explanation of this at length [may be found] in the book *Pardes [Rimmonim],* with God’s help.



## PART VII


### Subject

###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Subject 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Subject/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Subject/r/1)


בבאור קצת הכנויים בקצרה.


A Brief Explanation of Some [Divine] Appellations


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Subject 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Subject/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Subject/r/2)


והם אלו, ישמע חכם ויוסף לקח כי הקצור בזה לא ישביע נפש שוקקה רק התחלה קלה, להורות דרכי לשוננו המקודש שכלו רומז בעליונים, והארכנו בספר פרדס:


These are [Names] that *a wise man will hear and increase knowledge* (Prov. 1:5). Brevity in this [subject] will not satisfy the yearning soul. [It constitutes] but a modest beginning to teach the ways of our sanctified language, which in its entirety hints at upper [forces].¹ We have expatiated [on this] in the book *Pardes [Rimmonim]*.² sec. 23.

footnotes:
¹ Cf. Naḥmanides, who stated, in the introduction to his commentary on the Torah, that the Torah in its entirety is a divine Name.
² Pardes Rimmonim,



### Letter Alef

###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/1)


אב – יתייחס בכתר וגם בחכמה:


*Av* (“Father”) relates to Keter and also to Ḥokhmah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/2)


אב האמונה – יתייחס בכתר וחכמה מצד המלכות והבינה:


*Av ha-Emunah* (“Father of Faith”) relates to Keter and Ḥokhmah from the side of Malkhut and Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/3)


אב הרחמן – בתפארת מצד הכתר:


*Av ha-Raḥaman* (“Father of Mercy”) [relates to] Tiferet from the side of Keter.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/4)


אביר – בת״ת. אב״ן במלכות, ולה בחינות רבות:


*Abir* (“Mighty One”). Tiferet in a filial [relationship] with Malkhut. It has many aspects.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/5)


אבנים מפולמות – בחסד. ולפעמים בקליפה. בקליפה נגדיית:


*Avanim Mefulamot* (“Viscous Stones”). In Ḥesed and sometimes in the corresponding *kelipah*.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/6)


אבני תוהו – בחסד על צד הנזכר:


*Avne Tohu* (“Stones of the Void”). In Ḥesed on the aforementioned [*kelipah*] side.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/7)


אבוס – מקום יניקת החיות מהמלכות:


*Avus* (“Crib”). The place for deriving life from Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/8)


אבק – כנוי אל הקליפה:


*Avak* (“Dust”). Appellation of the *kelipah*.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/9)


אבקת רוכל – בבינה, וביסוד:


*Avak Rokhel* (“Powder of the Merchant”). In Binah and in Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/10)


אבר – יסוד:


*Ever* (“Member, Male Organ”). Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/11)


אברהם – חסד:


*Abraham,* Ḥesed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/12)


אגודה – כל האצילות במשמע:


*Agudah* (“Bunch”). All the emanation by implication.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/13)


אגוז – מלכות עם הקליפות ויסוד:


*Egoz* (“Nut”). Malkhut with the *kelipot* and Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/14)


אגן – המלכות בהיותה מלאה:


*Agan* (“Basin”). Malkhut when she is full.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/15)


אד – מלכות:


*Ed* (“Vapor”). Malkhut


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/16)


אדון – מלכות ויסוד:


*Adon* (“Master”). Malkhut and Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/17)


אדיר – בינה וגבורה:


*Adir*. (“Majestic”). Binah and Gevurah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/18)


אדרת – מלכות מצד הגבורה:


*Aderet* (“Mantle”). Malkhut from the side of Gevurah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/19)


אדם – חכמה ותפארת וכל האצילות:


*Adam* (“Man”), Ḥokhmah and Tiferet and all the emanation.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/20)


אדמה – המלכות מצד הדין:


*Adamah* (“Earth”). Malkhut from the side of Judgment.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/21)


אדמוני – הוד:


*Admoni* (“Ruddy”). Hod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/22)


אדני – מלכות:


*Adonai* (“Lord”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/23)


אדריכל – מלכות:


*Adrikhal* (“Architect”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/24)


אהבה – תחלתה מגבורה, וסופה בחסד, והיא מלכות:


*Ahavah* (“Love”). It begins from Gevurah and ends in Ḥesed. It is Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/25)


אה״ה – א׳‎ כתר, שנ״י ההי״ן בינה ומלכות:


*Ahah* (“Alas”). The *alef* [is] Keter and the two *hehs* [are] Binah and Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/26)


אהל מועד – מלכות משכן לתפארת:


*Ohel Moed* (“Tent of Meeting”). Malkhut as dwelling place of Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/27)


אוי – תפארת מסתלק אל הכתר לרעה:


*Oy* (“Woe”). Tiferet retreats to Keter for ill.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/28)


אויר – ת״ת וכתר. ויחוד חכמה ובינה:


*Avir* (“Air”). Tiferet and Keter; the union of Ḥokhmah and Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/29)


אוכל – צדיק:


*Okhel* (“Food”). The Righteous [Yesod].


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/30)


אומן – יסוד:


*Omen* (“Tutor”). Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/31)


און – הקליפה וכן כל לשון און ואונאה:


*Aven* (“Evil”). The *kelipah*. Thus with any form of iniquity or deceit.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/32)


אופן – הגדולה, ואופני המרכבה נצח הוד יסוד והמלכות:


*Ofan* (“Wheel”). Gedulah. The wheels of the chariot: Neẓaḥ, Hod, Yesod, and Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/33)


אור – בת״ת ובחסד ובבינה:


*Or* (“Light”). In Tiferet, Ḥesed, and Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/34)


אורח – מהכתר אל הת״ת, ואם הוא עקלתון בחיצונים:


*Oraḥ* (“Path”). From Keter to Tiferet. If it is crooked, [it refers to] the “outside” ones.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/35)


אות – יסוד, ולפעמים במלכות:


*Ot* (“Sign”). Yesod and sometimes in Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/36)


אז – חבור חכמה ובינה, וגם חבור תפארת במלכות:


*Az* (“Then”). The union of Ḥokhmah and Binah and also the union of Tiferet in Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/37)


אזוב – צדיק משפיע למלכות:


*Ezov* (“Hyssop”). The Righteous One influences Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/38)


אח – ת״ת למלכות מצד החסד, וכולל עד היסוד שהם ט׳‎:


*A*“ (“Brother”). Tiferet in relation to Malkhut from the side of Ḥesed, which includes [all the sefirot] until Yesod, which are nine.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/39)


אחד – ת״ת כולל ט׳‎ עם המלכות ד׳‎:


*Eḥad* (“One” [masc.]). Tiferet inclusive of the nine [sefirot]; with Malkhut four.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/40)


אחור – מלכות:


*Aḥor* (“Behind”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/41)


אחר הדברים – הקליפה והמקטרג:


*Aḥar ha-Devarim* (“After the Things”). The *kelipah* and the accusers.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/42)


אחרית – בינה:


*Aḥarit* (“End”). Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/43)


אחרית הימים – מלכות:


*Aḥarit ha-Yamim* (“End of Days”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/44)


אחת – בינה וחכמה וכתר:


*Aḥat* (“One” [fem.]). Binah, Ḥokhmah, and Keter.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/45)


אטד – מצד הדין הקשה והגבורה:


*Atad* (“Thorn Bush”). From the side of Rigorous Judgment and Gevurah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/46)


אי – חכמה ובינה:


*Ei* (“Where”). Ḥokhmah and Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/47)


אין – כתר כולל חכמה ובינה:


*Ayin* (“Nothing”). Keter inclusive of Ḥokhmah and Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/48)


אילת השחר ואילת אהבים – מלכות:


*Ayelet ha-Shaḥar* and *Ayelet Ahavim* (“Morning Star” and “Lovely Woman”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/49)


אין סוף – עלת כל העלות:


*Eyn Sof* (“Without End”). Cause of all Causes.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/50)


אי״ק – כתר חכמה בינה, או כתר תפארת מלכות:


*Eyk* (short for *Eyn Ketz*, “Without Finish”). Keter, Ḥokhmah, Binah or Keter, Tiferet, Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/51)


איש – תפארת כולל כתר חכמה בינה:


*Ish* (“Man”). Tiferet inclusive of Keter, Ḥokhmah, and Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/52)


אכתריאל – יש שפירשו במלכות, ולי נראה בבריאה:


*Akhatriel* (an angel). There are those who explain it [with reference] to Malkhut. It seems to me [to refer] to Creation.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/53)


אל – שהיא מלה שרתית כמו אספרה אל חק שאינו קדוש, בעולם הזכר:


*El* (“To”). This is a root word, as in *I will tell of* [*el*] *the decree* (Ps. 2:7), which is not holy. In the male world.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/54)


אלה – הם ג׳‎ אבות, או החצונים לפי הענין:


*Eleh* (“These”). They are the three Patriarchs³ or the “Outside” ones, according to context.

footnotes:
³ The sefirah Gedulah is associated with Abraham, Gevurah with Isaac, and Tiferet with Jacob.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/55)


אל – קדוש, ימצא בכתר ובחסד ובמלכות וביסוד:


*El* (“God”). Holy. It will be found in Keter and in Ḥesed and in Malkhut and in Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 56

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/56](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/56)


אלוה – א״ל חסד. ו״ה תפארת ומלכות:


*Eloha* (“Deity”), *alef, lamed* [=] Ḥesed; *vav, heh* [=] Tiferet and Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 57

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/57](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/57)


אלי – בינה כוללת ג׳‎ ראשונות:


*Eli* (“My God”). Binah inclusive of the three first [sefirot].


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 58

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/58](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/58)


אלישבע – מלכות יונקת מז׳‎ מדות:


*Elisheva* (feminine name). Malkhut deriving sustenance from the seven qualities.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 59

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/59](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/59)


אלוף – תפארת:


*Aluf* (“Chief”). Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 60

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/60](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/60)


אלקינו שבשמים – בינה:


*Elokenu she-ba-Shamayim* (“Our God in Heaven”). Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 61

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/61](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/61)


אלקים חיים – בינה ומלכות:


*Elokim Ḥayyim* (“Living God”). Binah and Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 62

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/62](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/62)


אלקיך – ‎בינה ומלכות:


*Elokekha* (“Your God”). Binah and Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 63

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/63](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/63)


אם – בינה ומלכות:


*Em* (“Mother”). Binah and Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 64

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/64](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/64)


אמה – תפארת, כוללת ו׳‎ טפחים, ו׳‎ ספירות:


*Amah* (“Cubit”). Tiferet inclusive of six *tefaḥim,*⁴ is a unit of measure., six sefirot.

footnotes:
⁴ The tefaḥ


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 65

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/65](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/65)


אמן – יסוד, מיחד תפארת ומלכות וכולל עשר:


*Amen* (“Amen”). Yesod uniting Tiferet and Malkhut and inclusive of the ten [sefirot].


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 66

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/66](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/66)


אמון – כתר ת״ת יסוד בינה, בריש בראשית רבה:


*Emon* (“Hidden”). Keter, Tiferet, Yesod, Binah. [Cf.] the beginning of Bereshit Rabbah.⁵

footnotes:
⁵ The verse from Prov. 8:30 containing this word begins the first section of Bereshit Rabbah (1:1).


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 67

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/67](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/67)


אמונה – בינה מצד החסד, מלכות מצד היסוד:


*Emunah* (“Faith”). Binah from the side of Ḥesed; Malkhut from the side of Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 68

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/68](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/68)


אומנת – בינה אל תפארת:


*Omenet* (“Nursemaid”). Binah in relation to Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 69

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/69](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/69)


אמוראים – גדולה גבורה תפארת:


*Amoraim* (sages of the Gemara). Gedulah, Gevurah, Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 70

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/70](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/70)


אמרת – מלכות:


*Imrat* (“The word of”) Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 71

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/71](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/71)


אמירה – במלכות מצד היותה למעלה:


The “word” is in Malkhut from the side of its being above.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 72

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/72](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/72)


אמ״ש – הם כתר חכמה בינה:


*Emesh* (“Yesterday Evening”). They are Keter, Ḥokhmah, Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 73

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/73](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/73)


אמת – תפארת. ויש בכללה תפארת מלכות יסוד:


*Emet* (“Truth”). Tiferet, sometimes inclusive of Tiferet, Malkhut, Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 74

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/74](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/74)


אנא – כתר בינה חכמה:


*Ana* (“Please”). Keter, Binah, Ḥokhmah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 75

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/75](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/75)


אנחה – מצד התפארת:


*Anaḥa* (“Tribulation”). From the side of Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 76

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/76](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/76)


אני – מלכות, בינה:


*Ani* (“I”). Malkhut, Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 77

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/77](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/77)


אניה – כחות המלכות, הם ספינות בים:


*Aniyah* (“Ship”). The powers of Malkhut are [as a] ship on the sea.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 78

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/78](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/78)


אנכי – בינה ומלכות:


*Anokhi* (“I”). Binah and Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 79

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/79](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/79)


אנפי רברבי – גדולה וגבורה. או כרובים עליונים:


*Anfei Ravrevei* (“Great Branches”). Gedulah and Gevurah or the high cherubs.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 80

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/80](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/80)


אנפי זוטרי – נצח הוד. או כרובים תחתונים:


*Anfei Zutrei* (“Lesser Branches”). Neẓaḥ, Hod or the lower cherubs.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 81

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/81](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/81)


אסטומכא – מלכות:


*Istumkhah* (“Stomach”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 82

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/82](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/82)


אספקלריא המאירה – ת״ת. ושאינה מאירה מלכות. וכן נצח והוד, אף מלכות מצד גבורה:


*Ispaklariah ha-Me’irah* (“Shining Mirror”). Tiferet. *[Ispaklariah] she-eina Me’irah* (“The Mirror That Does Not Shine”). Malkhut and also Neẓaḥ, Hod. Also, Malkhut from the side of Gevurah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 83

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/83](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/83)


אפיסת הרעיון – כתר:


*Afisat ha-Ra’ayon* (“Nullification of the Mind”). Keter.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 84

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/84](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/84)


אפיקי מים – נצח והוד:


*Afikei Mayim* (“Streams of Water”). Neẓaḥ, Hod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 85

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/85](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/85)


אפלה – גבורה:


*Afelah* (“Darkness”). Gevurah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 86

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/86](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/86)


אפרוח – תפארת ויסוד:


*Efroaḥ* (“Fledgling”). Tiferet and Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 87

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/87](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/87)


אפריון – מלכות כוללת בעשר:


*Apirion* (“Palanquin”). Malkhut inclusive of the ten [sefirot].


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 88

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/88](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/88)


אפרסמון – בינה כוללת שש קצוות חופפת על ארבע:


*Aparsimon* (“Persimmon”). Binah inclusive of the six “directions” covering four.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 89

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/89](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/89)


אצבעות י׳‎ – הם עשר ספירות חמשה הם – גדולה, גבורה, תפארת, נצח, והוד:


*Eẓbaot* (“Fingers”). Ten [indicate] the ten sefirot; five constitute Gedulah, Gevurah, Tiferet, Neẓaḥ, Hod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 90

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/90](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/90)


ארגמן – גדולה ת״ת גבורה מלכות:


*Argaman* (“Purple”). Tiferet, Gedulah, Gevurah, Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 91

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/91](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/91)


ארוך – תפארת:


*Arokh* (“Long”). Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 92

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/92](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/92)


ארון – מלכות:


*Aron* (“Ark”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 93

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/93](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/93)


ארזי לבנון – מציאות הספירות שבכתר:


*Arzei Levanon* (“Cedars of Lebanon”). The existence of the sefirot within Keter.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 94

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/94](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/94)


אריה – חסד:


*Aryeh* (“Lion”). Ḥesed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 95

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/95](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/95)


ארך אפים – תפארת מצד הכתר או כתר עצמו:


*Erekh ‘Apayim* (“Slow to Anger”). Tiferet from the aspect of Keter or Keter itself.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 96

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/96](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/96)


ארמנות – נצח והוד, ארמנות אל המלכות:


*Armonot* (“Palaces”). Neẓaḥ and Hod constitute the “palaces” for Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 97

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/97](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/97)


ארץ – מלכות והכל לפי שם הלווי:


*Ereẓ* (“Earth”). Malkhut. It is all according to the accompanying name.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 98

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/98](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/98)


אש – גבורה:


*Esh* (“Fire”). Gevurah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 99

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/99](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/99)


אשה – מלכות. וכן אשה:


*Ishah* (“Woman”). Malkhut. The same for *Isheh* (“Fire Offering”).


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 100

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/100](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/100)


אשמורת – נצח הוד:


*Ashmorot* (“Watches”). Neẓaḥ, Hod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 101

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/101](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/101)


אשישות – היחוד על ידי אש לבן ואש אדום. או גדולה גבורה ת״ת:


*Ashishot* (“Cake”). The union brought about by white fire and red fire; or Gedulah, Gevurah, Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 102

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/102](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/102)


אשכלות – נצח הוד:


*Eshkolot* (“Bunches of Grapes”). Neẓaḥ, Hod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 103

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/103](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/103)


אשר – בינה או תפארת:


*Osher* (“Happiness”). Binah or Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 104

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/104](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/104)


אשרה – מלכות בהיותה יונקת מן התפארת:


*Asherah* (“Grove”). Malkhut when it derives sustenance from Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 105

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/105](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/105)


את – מלכות כלילא מא׳‎ ועד ת׳‎:


*’Et* (Particle designating direct object). Malkhut which is crowned from *alef* to *tav*.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 106

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/106](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/106)


את – מלכות:


*’At* (“You” [fem.]). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 107

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/107](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/107)


אתה – ת״ת, ובחסד ומלכות וחכמה ובינה:


*’Atah* (“You” [masc.]). Tiferet and in Ḥesed and Malkhut and in Ḥokhmah and in Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 108

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/108](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/108)


אתנים – נצח הוד יסוד:


*Etanim* (“Mighty Ones”). Neẓaḥ, Hod, Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Alef 109

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/109](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Alef/r/109)


אתרוג – מלכות:


*Etrog* (“Citron”). Malkhut.

*Be’er* (“Well”) Malkhut.

בגד – מלכות:


*Beged* (Garment”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 3

בהו – גדולה:


*Bohu* (“Void”). Gedulah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/4)


בועז – הוד:


*Boaz*. Hod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 5
