בקר – חסד או יסוד:


*Bakar* (“Cattle”). Ḥesed or Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/6)


בטן – בינה:


*Betten* (“Belly”). Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/7)


בינה – נקראת היותה מתיחדת עם חכמה:


*Binah* is so called when she unites with Ḥokhmah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/8)


בית – מלכות ובינה:


*Bayit* (“House”). Malkhut and Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/9)


בית דין – מלכות, והיא של ג׳‎, עם גדולה גבורה ותפארת:


*Beyt Din* (“House of Judgment, Court”). Malkhut when she is [part of] three [sefirot] with Gedulah, Gevurah, and Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/10)


בכור – כתר וחכמה ותפארת:


*Bekhor* (“Firstborn”). Keter and Ḥokhmah and Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/11)


במות – גדולה גבורה תפארת:


*Bamot* (“High Places”). Gedulah, Gevurah, Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/12)


בן – תפארת:


*Ben* (“Son”). Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/13)


בנות ירושלים – הנשמות. או י״ב בקר שתחת הים:


*Benot Yerushalayim* (“Daughters of Jerusalem”). The souls or the twelve cattle under the sea.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/14)


בר – צדיק גולה ממקומו לבר:


*Bar* (“Outside”). The Righteous [Yesod] is exiled from his place to the “Outside.”


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/15)


ביצה – מלכות. ויש שפירשו בו שעורא רב״א דיו״ד עלאה חכמה: וכזית – שעורא זוטא דיו״ד תתאה מלכות:


*Beẓah* (“Egg”). Malkhut. There are those who have explained that the great measure [Egg]⁶ is a measure of bulk. It is a larger unit than the *zayit*. refers to the upper *yod,* Ḥokhmah, and the smaller measure of an Olive refers to the lower *yod,* Malkhut.

footnotes:
⁶ The beẓah


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/16)


ברד – החסד, וקרוב אני לומר שהוא גבורה שבחסד:


*Barad* (“Hail”). Ḥesed, and I am close to saying that it is Gevurah-within-Ḥesed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/17)


ברוך – כתר, או י׳‎ ספירות כלם, או יסוד. או מלכות:


*Barukh* (“Blessed”). Keter or the ten sefirot together or Yesod or Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/18)


ברזל – מלכות, או תפארת:


*Barzel* (“Iron”). Malkhut or Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/19)


בריחים – ה׳‎ ספירות, חסד, פחד, תפארת, נצח, הוד:


*Beriḥim* (“Bars”). Five sefirot: Ḥesed, Paḥad [Gevurah], Tiferet, Neẓaḥ, Hod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/20)


בריח תיכון – תפארת:


*Beriaḥ Tikhon* (“The Middle Bar”). Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/21)


ברייתא – מלכות. גולה ונודדת:


*Baraita⁷* (“Outside Tradition”). Malkhut in exile and wandering.

footnotes:
⁷ A baraita is a Tannaitic tradition which does not appear in the Mishnah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/22)


ברית – תפארת, או מלכות, או יסוד:


*Berit* (“Covenant”). Tiferet or Malkhut or Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/23)


בריכה – מלכות:


*Berekhah* (“Pool”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/24)


ברכה – מלכות מצד החסד:


*Berakhah* (“Blessing”). Malkhut from the side of Ḥesed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/25)


ברקים – מצד היסוד:


*Berakim* (“Thunders”) from the side of Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/26)


בשר – מלכות:


*Basar* (“Flesh”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/27)


בת – מלכות:


*Bat* (Daughter”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/28)


בתי גוואי – ג׳‎ ראשונות:


*Batei Gavaei* (“Innermost Houses”). The three first [sefirot].


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Bet 29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Bet/r/29)


בתי בראי – ז׳‎ מדות:


*Bataei Baraei* (“Outermost Houses”). The seven [lower] qualities.



### Letter Gimel

###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/1)


גאולה – בינה, או יסוד. נצח הוד יסוד מלכות, ד׳‎ גאולות:


*Geulah* (“Redemption”). Binah or Yesod; Neẓaḥ, Hod, Yesod, Malkhut [are] the four “redemptions.”⁸

footnotes:
⁸ This refers to the four verbs signifying redemption in Exod. 6:6: “I will bring you out… I will deliver you … I will redeem you … I will take you.”


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/2)


גאון – לפי ההמשכה. הנמשכת על המדה היא מתגאית:


*Geon* (“Pride”). According to the continuation that is consequent on the [connection with the sefirotic] quality, she [Malkhut] is “prideful”.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/3)


גאות הים – חוט של חסד על המלכות:


*Geut ha-Yam* (“Tide”). Thread of Ḥesed upon Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/4)


גבוה – עיקרו שאין סוף, ולפעמים בשאר המדות, כמו גבוה מעל גבוה שומר, מכל מקום אין גבהות אלא מתוספת שפעו:


*Gavoha* (“Height”). Essentially [referring] to Eyn Sof; sometimes to the rest of the qualities, as in *for one higher than the high watches* (Eccles. 5:7). In any event, the “Height” is only [evident] from the addition of his emanation.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/5)


גבול – מלכות:


*Gevul* (“Border”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/6)


גבורה – מדה״ד הקשה ולפעמים הבינה:


*Gevurah* (“Bravery”). The quality of harsh judgment; sometimes [refers to] Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/7)


גביר – מלכות:


Gevir (“Lord”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/8)


גבעות – בינה ומלכות:


*Geva’ot* (“Hills”). Binah and Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/9)


גג החופה – כתר:


*Gag ha-Ḥofeh* (“Covering Roof”). Keter.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/10)


גד – מלכות:


*Gad*. Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/11)


גדולה – חסד:


*Gedulah* (“Greatness”). Ḥesed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/12)


גוייה – יסוד ותפארת:


*Geviyah* (“Body”). Yesod and Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/13)


גולגולת – ג׳‎ ראשונות:


*Gulgolet* (“Skull”). The three first [sefirot].


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/14)


גולות הכותרות – חסד וגבורה. והיסוד גולות הזהב:


*Gulot ha-Kotarot* (“Bowls of the Capitals” [I Kings 7:41]). Ḥesed and Gevurah; Yesod is the “Golden Bowl” (Eccles. 12:6).


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/15)


גיד הנשה – יסוד עם המלכות:


*Gid ha-Nasheh* (“Sciatic Nerve”). Yesod with Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/16)


גיהנם – גבורה מצד החצונים:


*Gehinnom* (“Gehenna”). Gevurah from the side of the “Outside” ones.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/17)


גי חזיון – מלכות:


*Ge Ḥizzayon* (“Valley of Vision” [Isa. 22:4]). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/18)


גל נעול – מלכות:


*Gal Na’ul* (“A Spring Shut Up” [Song of Songs 4:12]). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/19)


גלגלי המרכבה – נצח והוד:


*Galgalei ha-Merkavah* (“Wheels of the Chariot”). Neẓaḥ and Hod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/20)


גלים – כחות שבמלכות ושבבינה:


*Galim* (“Heaps”). Forces in Malkhut and Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/21)


גמל – הקליפה:


*Gamal* (“Camel”). The *Kelipah*.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/22)


גמילות חסדים – שפע החסד:


*Gemilut Ḥasadim* (“Acts of Lovingkindness”). The overflow of Ḥesed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/23)


גן – בינה ומלכות:


*Gan* (“Garden”). Binah and Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/24)


גפן – מלכות:


*Gefen* (“Vine”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Gimel 25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Gimel/r/25)


גר – ת״ת, בהיותו חוץ למקומו:


*Ger* (“Stranger”). Tiferet when it is out of its place.



### Letter Daled

###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/1)


דבור – מלכות:


*Dibbur* (“Speech”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/2)


דבר – מלכות:


*Davar* (“Word”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/3)


דבש – מצד הגבורה:


*Devash* (“Honey”). From the side of Gevurah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/4)


דו פרצופין – תפארת ומלכות:


*Du Parẓufin* (“Dual Figure”). Tiferet and Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/5)


דוד נאמן – יסוד:


*Dod Ne’eman* (“Faithful Love”). Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/6)


דוד – מלכות:


*David*. Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/7)


דור – יסוד ותפארת, או בינה ומלכות, וזהו לדור דור:


*Dor* (“Generation”). Yesod and Tiferet; or Binah and Malkhut. This is [what is meant by the expression] *to all generations* (Exod. 3:15).


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/8)


דיו – היא בבינה, הנותנת לוא״ו שבשם די ספוקו, ובה כתיבת ספר תורה, בסוד אש שחורה ע״ג אש לבנה:


*Dyo* (“Ink”). In Binah, which gives the *vav* in the [divine] Name its due nourishment. With it is written the Torah scroll in the mystery of “black fire on top of white fire.”⁹

footnotes:
⁹ Yerushalmi, Shekalim 6:1.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/9)


דיין – גבורה, או ת״ת מצד המלכות:


*Dayyan* (“Judge”). Gevurah or Tiferet from the side of Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/10)


דין תורה – דין אמת – מלכות:


*Din Torah* (“Judgment of the Torah”). True judgment; Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/11)


דך – יסוד:


*Dakh* (“Oppressed”). Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/12)


דכים – מלכות מצד היסוד:


*Dakhim* (“Oppressed” [pl.]). Malkhut from the side of Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/13)


דל – יסוד:


*Dal* (“Poor”). Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/14)


דם – סיתום השפע בבינה, שלא בא אל המלכות, לפיכך באה דל״ת דלה, ואצלה מ״ס סתומה:


*Dam* (“Blood”). The closing of the emanation in Binah so that it does not come to Malkhut. Therefore it comes as *she has surely drawn* (Exod. 2:19), and with her the *mem* is closed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/15)


דמיון – מלכות:


*Dimayon* (“Imagination”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/16)


דמעות – גבורה הוד:


*Dema’ot* (“Tears”). Gevurah, Hod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/17)


דעת – תפארת או יסוד בבחינה דקה:


*Da’at* (“Knowledge”). Tiferet or Yesod in the delicate aspect.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/18)


דר – מלכות, או בינה:


*Dar* (“Dwells”). Malkhut or Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/19)


דרור – בינה:


*Deror* (“Freedom”). Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/20)


דרום – חסד:


*Darom* (“South”). Ḥesed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Daled 21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Daled/r/21)


דרכים – צנורות השפע, מג׳‎ אבות אל המלכות:


*Derakhim* (“Paths”). The channels of the emanation from the three “patriarchs” to Malkhut.



### Letter Heh

###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/1)


הא – עצמה, בבואה בראש תיבה, הרמז לבינה. ובבואה בסוף תיבה, הרמז למלכות:


*Heh* (the letter). When it comes at the beginning of a word, it hints at Binah, and when it comes at the end of a word the hint is to Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/2)


הבל – ז׳‎ הבלים, ז׳‎ מדות:


*Hevel* (“Vanity”). Seven “Vanities” are the seven [lower sefirotic] qualities.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/3)


הגדה – מצד החכמה:


*Haggadah* (“Recital”). From the side of Ḥokhmah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/4)


הדום – מלכות:


*Hadom* (“Footstool”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/5)


הדס – ג׳‎ אבות, ומלכות הדסה:


*Hadas* (“Myrtle”). The three “patriarchs.” Malkhut [is called] *Hadassah*.¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ Hadassah was Esther’s name. Cf. Esther 2:7.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/6)


הדר – בתפארת או בנצח:


*Hadar* (“Glory”). In Tiferet or Neẓaḥ.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/7)


הוא – בכתר, או בבינה, או בתפארת, או במלכות:


*Hu* (“He”). In Keter or in Binah or in Tiferet or in Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/8)


הוד – נקרא כן, בהיותה נאחזת בגבורה ובינה:


*Hod* (“Majesty”). It is so called when it is held by Gevurah and Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/9)


הוה – רמז ה׳‎ עלאה, ה׳‎ תתאה, ו׳‎ באמצעיתא:


*Havah* (“To Exist”). Hints at the upper *heh,* the lower *heh,* and the *vav* in the middle.¹¹

footnotes:
¹¹ This is a reference to the three sefirot, Gedulah, Gevurah, and Tiferet. Cf. sec. 6, chap. 5, n. 29 above.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/10)


הוי – תפארת מסתלק עם הברכות אל הבינה:


*Havi* (“Existence”). Tiferet removes itself with the blessings to Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/11)


היא – מלכות:


*Hi* (“She”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/12)


היכל – מלכות:


*Heikhal* (“Palace”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/13)


הלכה – מלכות:


*Halakhah* (“Law”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/14)


הלל – מלכות, שאינו גמור, וגמור מצד הבינה, או בינה עצמה:


*Hallel* (Psalms of Praise). Malkhut when it is not completed.¹² Completed [it refers] to the side of Binah or to Binah herself.

footnotes:
¹² Hallel is a series of psalms of praise recited in the synagogue on various holidays. On some of them, not all the psalms are recited and the Hallel is considered “incomplete.”


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/15)


הללויה – מלכות מצד התפארת:


*Halleluyah* (“Praise the Lord”). Malkhut from the side of Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/16)


הנני – מלכות:


*Hinneni* (“Here am I”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/17)


הר – מלכות, והכל לפי שם המתלוה:


*Har* (“Mountain”). Malkhut. All is in accordance with the [divine] Name which accompanies [it].


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Heh 18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Heh/r/18)


התקפתא – מלכות מצד החסד:


*Hatkafah* (“Attack”). Malkhut from the side of Ḥesed.



### Letter Vav

###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Vav 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/1)


ו׳‎ בראש תיבה, מורה על התפארת, כענין ואני, ת״ת ומלכות:


*Vav* (the letter). At the beginning of a word it indicates Tiferet. Thus *V’ani* (“And I”) [indicates] Tiferet and Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Vav 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/2)


וה׳‎ – הוא ובית דינו:


*Vh*. He and His court.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Vav 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/3)


והו – תפארת:


*Vhu*. Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Vav 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/4)


וי – תפארת מסלק הברכות מיסוד אליו:


*Vay* (“Woe”). Tiferet removing the blessings from Yesod to him.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Vav 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/5)


ויהי – מצד הגבורה:


*Vayehi* (“And it came to pass”). From the side of Gevurah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Vav 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/6)


וילון – מלכות:


*Vilon* (“Curtain”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Vav 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/7)


ועד – בינה שהיא מקבצת הספירות:


*Va’ad* (“Assembly”). Binah, for she assembles the sefirot.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Vav 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Vav/r/8)


ושט – כחות תחת המלכות נקראים אישים:


*Veset* (“Period”). The powers under Malkhut which are called *Ishim.*¹³ Hilkhot Yesodei ha-Torah 2:7.

footnotes:
¹³ Ishim is the lowest order of angels. Cf. Maimonides, Mishneh Torah

זית – מלכות. ולפעמים שבע מדות כלם זתים:


*Zayit* (“Olive”). Malkhut. Sometimes the seven [lower sefirotic] qualities are all [called] “olives.”

*Zekhirah* (“Remembrance”). From the side of Yesod.

*Ḥomah* (“Wall”). Malkhut.

חוסן – תפארת:


*Ḥosen* (“Riches”). Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/17)


חופה – כתר, גג שלה חכמה, וכותלה בינה. פתחיה, גדולה וגבורה.עמודיה, נצח והוד. ותחתיה חתן כלה ושושבין, תפארת ומלכות ויסוד:


*Ḥuppah* (“Canopy”). Keter. Its roof is Ḥokhmah; its walls are Binah; its doors are Gedulah and Gevurah; its pillars are Neẓaḥ and Hod; underneath it are the bridegroom, the bride, and the attendant: Tiferet and Malkhut and Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/18)


חוקקי ישראל – אבא ואמא מאצילי התפארת:


*Ḥokkekei Yisrael* (“Legislators of Israel”). “Father” and “Mother”¹⁵ from the emanators of Tiferet.

footnotes:
¹⁵ Ḥokhmah and Binah.

*Ḥazu* (“Foretelling”). Of the night; Gabriel.

*Ḥorin* (“Free”). Ḥokhmah and Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/35)


חלב – שפע הנשפע מכתר, דרך החכמה אל הבינה:


*Ḥalav* (“Milk”). The abundance which emanates from Keter through Ḥokhmah to Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/36)


חלה – מלכות:


*Ḥallah* (“Consecrated Dough”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/37)


חלום – המלכות מצד עצמה:


*Ḥalom* (“Dream”). Malkhut from her own side.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/38)


חלון – תפארת:


*Ḥalon* (“Window”). Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/39)


חלצים – יחוד התפארת עם המלכות, בין נצח והוד:


*Ḥalaẓaim* (“Loins”). The union of Tiferet with Malkhut between Neẓah and Hod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/40)


חם – מצד הגבורה:


*Ḥam* (“Warm”). From the side of Gevurah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/41)


חמדת הימים – היא השכינה:


*Ḥemdat Yamim* (“Love of Life”). She is the Shekhinah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/42)


חמה – תפארת:


*Ḥammah* (“Sun”). Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/43)


חמשה – הרמז לתפארת, או מלכות:


*Ḥamishah* (“Five”). The hint is to Tiferet or Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/44)


חנון – בחסד, והעיקר בבינה. חסד במקומו, ובכתר, ובנצח ובהוד, ואלו הם:


*Ḥanun* (“Merciful”). In Ḥesed and the essence in Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/45)


כתר חסד עלאה, נצח הוד חסדי דוד:


*Ḥessed* (“Kindness”). In its place and in Keter and in Neẓah and in Hod. They are: Keter [is] the upper Ḥesed. Neẓaḥ, Hod [are] “The Kindnesses of David.”¹⁸

footnotes:
¹⁸ Cf. Isa. 55:3.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/46)


חסיד – חכמה בחסד:


*Ḥassid* (“Pious”). Ḥokhmah in Ḥesed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/47)


חסידה – בינה על חסד, או מלכות מצד חסד:


*Ḥassidah* (“Stork”). Binah over Ḥesed or Malkhut from the side of Ḥesed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/48)


חפץ – מלכות מצד הת״ת בשפע הכתר:


*Ḥafeẓ* (“Possession”). Malkhut from the side of Tiferet in the emanation of Keter.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/49)


חפשי – בינה:


*Ḥofshi* (“Free”). Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/50)


חצות לילה – מלכות מצד הרחמים:


*Ḥaẓẓot Laylah* (“Midnight”). Malkhut from the side of Mercy.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/51)


חצי – מצד המלכות שהיא מצה פרוסה:


*Ḥaẓẓi* (“Half”). From the side of Malkhut which is a portion of Matzah.¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁹ This is a reference to the ceremonial breaking of a matzah at the Passover Seder.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/52)


חצים – מצד היסוד:


*Ḥizzim* (“Arrows”). From the side of Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/53)


חצוצרות כסף – נצח והוד מצד החסד:


*Ḥaẓẓoẓrot Kessef* (“Silver Trumpets”). Neẓaḥ and Hod from the side of Ḥesed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/54)


חצרות ה׳‎ – ז׳‎ מדות:


*Ḥaẓerot Ha[Shem]* (“Courts of the Lord”). The seven [lower sefirotic] qualities.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/55)


חק – יסוד:


*Ḥok* (“Law”). Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 56

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/56](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/56)


חקה – מלכות:


*Ḥukkah* (“Decree”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 57

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/57](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/57)


חקות – כחות המלכות למטה:


*Ḥukkot* (“Laws”). The powers of Malkhut below.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 58

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/58](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/58)


חקר – מלכות:


*Ḥeker* (“Inquiry”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 59

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/59](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/59)


חרב – גבורה, והעיקר כל יהו״ה, או מלכות:


*Ḥerev* (“Sword”). Gevurah and essentially all YDVH or Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 60

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/60](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/60)


חשך – במלכות ובגבורה ובכתר, ובחוץ:


*Ḥoshekh* (“Darkness”). In Malkhut and in Gevurah and in Keter and in the “Outside.”


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 61

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/61](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/61)


חשמל – נצח הוד, וסוד יאהדונה״י:


*Ḥashmal* (“Electrum”). Neẓaḥ, Hod; the mystery of [the Name] YAHDVNHY.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 62

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/62](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/62)


חשקיהם – נצח הוד:


*Ḥeshkehem* (“Their Desire”). Neẓaḥ, Hod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Chet 63

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/63](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Chet/r/63)


חתן – תפארת:


*Ḥatan* (“Bridegroom”). Tiferet.



### Letter Tet

###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tet 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/1)


טבור – מלכות מצד הבינה:


*Tabur* (“Navel”). Malkhut from the side of Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tet 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/2)


טבעת – מלכות החכמה, והעיקר סוד ם׳‎ סתומה, בינה שבחכמה:


*Taba’at*(“Ring”). Malkhut [from the side] of Ḥokhmah. The essential secret of the closed *mem* [Isa. 9:6]; Binah in Ḥokhmah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tet 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/3)


טהרה – מצד החסד, או מצד הגבורה:


*Taharah* (“Purification”) from the side of Ḥesed or from the side of Gevurah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tet 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/4)


טוב – בחסד, או בכתר, או ביסוד:


*Tov* (“Good” [masc.]). In Ḥesed or in Keter or in Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tet 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/5)


טובה – מלכות:


*Tovah* (“Good” [fem.]). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tet 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/6)


טוב ירושלים – על מלכות מתפארת:


*Tuv Yerushalayim* (“The Good of Jerusalem” [Ps. 128:5]). Concerning Malkhut from Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tet 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/7)


טחול – חצוני:


*Taḥol* (“Spleen”). “Outside.”


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tet 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/8)


טחנות – נצח והוד. או כחות תחת המלכות. נקראים אישים:


*Taḥanot* (“Stations”). Neẓaḥ and Hod, or the [angelic] powers under Malkhut called *Ishim*.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tet 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/9)


טל – בתפארת, כללות יו״ד ה״א וא״ו:


*Tal* (“Dew”). In Tiferet the combination of *yod, heh, vav*.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tet 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/10)


טלית לבנה – תפארת מצד החסד. ויצדק במקומות רבים:


*Tallit* (“Garment, Prayer Garment”). Moon; Tiferet from the side of Ḥesed. It is appropriate in many places.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tet 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/11)


טנא – מלכות:


*Tene* (“Basket”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tet 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/12)


טפה – מלכות:


*Tipah* (“Drop”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tet 13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Tet/r/13)


טפח – מלכות מצד חמשה הספירות:


*Tefaḥ* (“Span”). Malkhut from the side of the five sefirot.



### Letter Yod

###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/1)


יאור – יסוד:


*Y’or* (“Nile”). Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/2)


יבק – גדולה גבורה תפארת, ג׳‎ קוין, קו דין, קו רחמים, קו חסד:


*Yabbok* (Jabbok). Gedulah, Gevurah, Tiferet; three paths, the path of Judgment, the path of Mercy, the path of Lovingkindness.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/3)


יבשה – מלכות:


*Yabashah* (“Dry Land”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/4)


יד – מלכות, או בינה. מצד הגבורה – היד החזקה. מצד התפארת – יד רמה. מצד החסד – יד הגדולה:


*Yad* (“Hand”). Malkhut or Binah; From the side of Gevurah. *Yod ha-Ḥazakah* (“Strong Hand”). From the side of Tiferet. *Yad Ramah* (“Upraised Hand”). From the side of Ḥesed. *Yad ha-Gedolah* (“Great Hand”).


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/5)


ידיעה – לשון חבור זכר ונקבה:


Knowledge, signifying the union of male and female.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/6)


יהי – תפארת:


*Yehi* (“It Will Be”). Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/7)


יובל – בינה:


*Yovel* (“Jubilee”). Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/8)


יום – תפארת. ולפעמים במקומות אחרים, כגון חסד ובינה, והוא לפי הלווי:


*Yom* (“Day”). Tiferet and sometimes in its place others like Ḥesed and Binah according to the context.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/9)


יומם – חסד:


*Yomam* (“Daily”). Ḥesed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/10)


יונה – מלכות:


*Yonah* (“Dove”). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/11)


יוסף – יסוד:


*Yosef* (Joseph). Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/12)


יועץ – בינה:


*Yo’eẓ* (“Advisor”). Binah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 13

יוצר בראשית – חכמה:


*Yoẓer Bereshit* (“Creator”). Ḥokhmah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 14

יותרת הכבד – אשת זנונים:


*Yoteret ha-Kaved* (“Membrane on the Liver”). The woman of harlotry.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/15)


יחוד – תלוי בתפארת ומלכות. ויתלוי אליהם כל המדות:


*Yiḥud* (“Union”). Refers to Tiferet and Malkhut, and all the sefirot accompany them.

יכין – נצח:


*Yakhin* (“Pillar”). Neẓaḥ.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/17)


ילדים – נצח והוד:


*Yeladim* (“Children”). Neẓaḥ and Hod.

ים – בינה או חכמה:


*Yam* (“Seal”). Binah or Ḥokhmah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 19

ימים – ז׳‎ מדות. מקדם, באריך אנפין:


*Yamim* (“Days”). The seven [lower] sefirot. Previously in *arikh anpin*.²⁰

²⁰ An aspect of the sefirah Keter.

ימין – חסד. ולפעמים נצח, או חכמה:


*Yamin* (“Right”). Ḥesed, and sometimes Neẓaḥ or Ḥokhmah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 21

יעקב – מלכות, ומלא בת״ת:


*Ya’akov* (Jacob). Malkhut filled with [flow from] Tiferet.

יפה נוף – תפארת, או יסוד:


*Yefe Nof*’ (“Beauteous View”). Tiferet or Yesod.

יפה עינים – מלכות, מצד רוחצות בחלב:


*Yefe Enayim* (“Beautiful to the Eyes”). Malkhut from the side where they wash with milk.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/24)


יצחק – גבורה:


*Yiẓḥak* (Isaac). Gevurah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 25

יראה – גבורה או בינה, או מלכות:


*Yir’eh* (“He Will See”). Gevurah or Binah or Malkhut.

ירום – בינה מצד החכמה:


*Yarum* (“He Will Raise Up”). Binah from the side of Ḥokhmah.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/27)


ירושלים – מלכות:


*Yerushalayim* (Jerusalem). Malkhut.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/28)


ירח – מלכות:


*Yare’aḥ* (“Moon”). Malkhut.

יריעה – תפארת:


*Yeriah* (“Curtain”). Tiferet.

ירכתי צפון – נצח והוא מצד הגבורה:


*Yarketey Ẓafon* (“Furthest Place in the North”). Neẓaḥ and Hod from the side of Gevurah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Yod 31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/31)


יש – חכמה בשתוף בינה:


*Yesh* (“Exists”). Ḥokhmah in partnership with Binah.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/32)


ישועה – מצד החסד:


*Yeshuah* (“Salvation”). From the side of Ḥesed.

ישורון – ת״ת, כולל ימין ושמאל:


*Yeshurun* (Jeshurun). Tiferet, inclusive of Right and Left.

ישיבה של מעלה – בינה:


*Yeshivah shel Ma’alah* (“Court on High”). Binah.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/35)


ישר – מלכות מתייחדת עם הת״ת:


*Yashar* (“Upright”). Malkhut together with Tiferet.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/36)


ישני עפר – ג׳‎ אבות במלכות:


*Yeshenei Afar* (“Those Who Sleep in the Dust”). The three “Patriarchs” in Malkhut.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Yod/r/37)


ישראל – תפארת מכריע בין חכמה ובינה:


*Yisrael* (Israel). Tiferet as decisive force between Ḥokhmah and Binah.



### Letter Kaf

###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Kaf 1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/1)


כבד – הזכר שבקליפה, יותרת הכבד לילית:


*Kabed* (“Liver”). The male [partner] of the *kelipah*. [Cf.] *Yoteret ha-Kaved—*Lilith.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Kaf 2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/2)


כבוד – השכינה שהיא ביחוד היסוד:


*Kavod* (“Glory”). The Shekhinah joined with Yesod.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Kaf 3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/3)


כבודי – בינה, והכל בסוד ל״ב נתיבות:


*Kevodi* (“My Glory”). Binah and all is in the mystery of the thirty-two paths.²¹ 1:1.

footnotes:
²¹ Cf. Sefer Yeḥirah

כבוש – תפארת:


*Kevosh* (“Conquer”). Tiferet.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Kaf 5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/5)


כביר – גבורה:


*Kabir* (“Mighty”). Gevurah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Kaf 6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/6)


כבר – כתר בינה ראשית:


*Kevar* (name of river [see. Ezek. 1:2]). Keter, Binah, Reshit [= Ḥokhmah].


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Kaf 7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/7)


כבשים – הז׳‎ מדות, או מצד הבינה, או החסד:


*Kevasim* (“Sheep”). The seven [lower] sefirot either from the side of Binah or of Ḥesed.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Kaf 8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/8)


כבשן – מלכות אופה ומבשלת הזרע:


*Kivshan* (“Furnace”). Malkhut which is covering and “cooking” the seed.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/9)

*Kad* (“Pitcher”). Yesod.

כדה – מלכות:


*Kada* (“Her Pitcher”). Malkhut.

*Koh* (“Thus”). Malkhut or Binah.

*Kohen* (“Priest”). Ḥesed.

כובע ישועה – אין סוף, או תפארת, או מלכות:


*Kova Yeshuah* (“Hat of Salvation”). Eyn Sof or Tiferet or Malkhut.

*Kos Shel Berakhah* (“Goblet of Benediction”). Malkhut from the side of her hosts.

*Kos Yeshuot* (“Goblet of Salvation”). [Malkhut] in her joining with the salvations.

*Kotel* (“Wall”). Tiferet with Malkhut at the hand of Yesod.

כיור וכנו – נצח והוד:


*Kiyor ve-Kano* (“Basin and Its Pedestal”). Neẓaḥ and Hod.

כח – בינה מצד החכמה:


*Koaḥ* (“Strength”). Binah from the side of Ḥokhmah.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Kaf 19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/19)

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/PART%20VII,_Letter%20Kaf/r/21)

*Kelayot* (“Kidneys”). Neẓaḥ and Hod.

כנפי העין – נצח והוד:


*Kanfei ha-Ain* (“The Wings of the Eye”). Neẓaḥ and Hod.

*Kessef* (“Silver”). Ḥesed.

*Kaf Zekhut* (“Merit”). Ḥesed.

###### Ohr Ne'erav, PART VII, Letter Tav 30

ותשלם מלאכת שמים זו ביום ד׳‎ בזב״ד טוב א״ב הרחמן ירבה להשיב אפו וארמון ישב על משפטו:


This work of heaven was completed on the fourth day [Wednesday], the *goodly gift* (Gen. 30:20) [= 13th] of Av the Merciful, [in the year] May [God] greatly restrain [*le-hashiv =* 347 (i.e., 5347, or 1587 in the civil calendar)] His anger and establish the Palace upon its rightful place.²⁴

footnotes:
²⁴ The “work of heaven” refers to the printing of the book, which was completed on Wednesday, 13 Av, 5347.

**TITLE PAGE**

ספר


The book


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/1:3)


**אור נערב**


*The Pleasant Light*


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/1:4)


מהרב המקובל האלדי כמוהר"ר **משה קורדואירו ז"ל**


[written] by the rabbi, the divine kabbalist, his honor, our teacher, rabbi Moses Cordovero, may his memory be a blessing.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/1:5)


מתוק לנפש ומרפא לעצם מהדעות הזרות המתוארות למתרחקים מחכמת האמתי זה השער לה' לזכות אח"כ לכספ' אור יקר הוא המאור הגדול בפי' הזוהר:


[The book] is sweet for the soul and a healing for the bone, [a remedy] against the strange opinions described [therein] of those who distance themselves from the science of truth. *This is the gate of the Lord* (Ps. 118:20) in order that [we may] merit afterwards [the printing of] the book *‘Or Yakar,*¹ was not published until the twentieth century (Jerusalem, 1965 et seq.). which is the great light concerning the commentary of the Zohar.

footnotes:
¹ Cordovero’s commentary on the Zohar, Or Yakar,


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/1:6)


והחלק הששי מהחבור הזה הוא קצור משובח ומפואר מההקדמות שבאר הרב זצ"ל בארוכה בימי בבחרותו בספר **פרדס רמונים** שלו:


The sixth section of this book is a glorious and praiseworthy abbreviation of the opinions which the rabbi, may his righteous memory be a blessing, dealt with at length in his youth in his book *Pardes Rimmonim*.² at the age of twenty-seven.

footnotes:
² Cordovero completed Pardes Rimmonim


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:1)


**הקדמת ר' משה באסולה**


**INTRODUCTION BY MOSES BASSOLA**


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:2)


אמר הצעיר משה בסולה


Said the young man, Moses Bassola:¹ as well as Moses Cordovero’s ethical treatise *Tomer Devorah* (Venice, 1589).

footnotes:
¹ Bassola was a sixteenth-century Kabbalist, originally from Safed, who settled in Italy and collaborated with Gedaliah Cordovero on the publication of Or Ne’erav


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:3)


עלי לשבח אל החכם כמהר' גדליה בן הרב המחבר זצ"ל כי שמר ברית אחים ואהבתו הקדומה בארץ הצבי תוב"ב עקד שהביאני אל הדר תורתו והעמידני על ימינו לעזרו לסמכו ולתמכו להרים מכשול מדרך הספר הנכבד הלז


It is incumbent upon me to praise the sage, his honor, our teacher, Rabbi Gedaliah,² son of the rabbi who authored [this work], may his righteous memory be a blessing. For he has kept the covenant of brotherhood and his old friendship [with me] in the Land of the Hart [Israel], may it be speedily rebuilt and established. He has brought me into the chamber of his learning and has stood me at his right hand to help and support him in order to raise the stumbling block from the path of this important book.

footnotes:
² Gedaliah Cordovero (1562-1625), son of Moses Cordovero, was born in Safed. In Italy he collaborated with Moses Bassola and Menaḥem Azariah of Fano in the publication of various Kabbalistic treatises, including several by his father.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:4)


והחוט המשולש עמנו ה"ה הישיש ונעלה רבי ישראל עושה חיל ברב עוז ותעצומות במלאכת הדפוס לשם שמים ובאמונה באופן כי בטחנו בחסד אל יהיה מוגה כהלכתו כפי' סדר עבודת יום הכפורים וכל שלו ושחיתה לא ישתכח ביה.


The *threefold thread* (Eccles. 4:12) with us is the great sage, the venerable and exalted Rabbi Israel, who works successfully with great might and power in the labor of printing honestly and for the sake of heaven. We have trusted, therefore, with God’s mercy, that he will proofread properly, as he did in [the book] *Seder ‘Avodat Yom ha-Kippurim,*³ which was all his [work]. Mistakes are not found with him.

footnotes:
³ This was published in Venice in 1587.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:5)


וליודעים ינעם ועליהם תבא ברכת שמים מעל:


To those who know may God be pleasant. May the blessing of heaven above come upon them.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:6)


ואלה הדברים אשר דבר משה בסולה בראותו החבור ומעלתו ואת יקר תפארת גדולתו:


These are the words which Moses Bassola spoke when he saw [this] composition and its value and the *honor of* [its] *excellent majesty (Esther* 1:4):


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:7)


כל ישראל יש להם חלק בעולם הנשמות החשובות חצובות מתחת כסא עושה ארץ בכחו ובתבונתו נטה שמים. וזאת הברכה אשר ברך משה איש האלדים את בני ישראל לטהר רעיונים ולשית בטחות חכמה לאור באור החיים ונעלמה מעיני כל חי. עד עת בא החכם בנו יצ"ו והוציא חמה ממקומה להאיר לארץ ולדרים באור חברוהו שרים. קראוהו נדיבי העם טעמו וראו מתקו וערכו. וכל איש ישאנו בחשקו בחיקו:


All Israel has a place in the world of worthy souls which are cut out from under the throne⁴ 24a. of Him who made the earth with His power and understanding, who *stretched forth the heavens* (Isa.44:24). *This is the blessing with which Moses, the man of God,*⁵ *blessed the children of Israel* (Deut. 33:1) in order to purify their thoughts and to securely establish wisdom, and *to be enlightened with the light of the living* (Job 33:30). [However,] it was hidden from the eyes of the living until his son the sage,⁶ may his Rock and Redeemer protect him, came and brought forth the sun from its place to bring light to the earth and those who dwell [there]. It is a treatise which princes have written. The benefactors of the people have said of it: Taste and see its sweetness and its worth. Every man should bear it in his desire and his bosom.

footnotes:
⁴ Cf. Zohar Ḥadash
⁵ A reference to Moses Cordovero.
⁶ A reference to Gedaliah Cordovero.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:8)


וזה חלקי מכל עמלי. להביא תשורת שירת מהללי.


*This is my portion from all my labor* (Eccles. 2:10) to bring as a gift my poem of praise:


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:9)


לכו נא זמירי רנני ושירי


Go now, my songs, exultations, and poems


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:10)


להלל לפאר לעושה מאורים:


To praise and extol Him who formed abundances


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/2:11)


ונטע בחכמה שלחיו **בפרדס**


And planted with wisdom His messengers in the Garden⁷.

footnotes:
⁷ A reference to Cordovero’s Pardes Rimmonim

**הקדמת ר' גדליה קורדואירו**


**INTRODUCTION BY GEDALIAH CORDOVERO**


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:2)


אמר הצעיר גדליה בן לא"א כמהור"ר משה קורדואירו זצ"ל


Said the young man Gedaliah, son of my lord, my father, his honor, our teacher Rabbi Moses Cordovero, may his righteous memory be a blessing:


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:3)


מאז הפלא ה' חסיד לו זאת הקדוש אבא מרי והקריב אליו להגדיל תורה להפליא תושיה בחכמת הרזים ראינו צדקתו עומדת לעד להלהיב לבות המעיינים ונפקחו עיניהם בהקדמותיו האלהיות לא זזו מחבתן עד שזכו לנעור כפיהם מתמוך בחכמות המחקריות אשר דרכם חשך וחלקלקות וצדיקים ישמחו יעלצו בחכמת האמת להתהלך לפני אלדים באור החיים:


Since the time when God wondrously showed His lovingkindness to the holy man, my master and father, and came near to him in order to make the Torah great and to wondrously grant wisdom in the secret science [of Kabbalah], we have seen that his righteousness endures forever, encouraging the hearts of the students. Their eyes have been opened by his divine treatises. Their love [for them] continued until they merited to cease supporting the philosophical sciences, whose ways are darkness and sophistry. The righteous will be glad and rejoice in the science of the truth [so as to] walk before the Lord in the light of life.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:4)


ומעתה הקונטריס הזה שהוא סלת נקיה מנופה בנפת הרב אבא מרי בעצמו ובכבודו בקצור ההקדמות שנתבארו בארוכה בספר **פרדס רמונים** אשר לו ועם פרקים נוספים בתחלתו להבין ולהורות תועלת החכמה והכרח למודה ראיתי היותו מצוי ביד רבים נכבדי ארץ בשגם יודע אני כי לא עשה לו הרב מהדורא מספקת כאשר היה עם לבבו ויראתי פן יקרהו כמקרה פירוש תפלת ר"ה כי גונב מעמדי ומי שהדפיסו בלי רשותי לא ירד לסוף דעת הרב המחבר ולא די שלא תקן מה שנמצא בגנבתו מטעות סופר אלא שהשמיט וגרע ואני מובטח ברחמי שמים שאזכה לתקן המעוות ב"ה.


This treatise is fine flour free of dirt which was sifted by the rabbi, my father and master, himself. He abbreviated the arguments which were expounded at length in the book *Pardes Rimmonim* which he wrote. He also included additional chapters at its beginning to cause to understand and to teach the usefulness of the science [of Kabbalah] and the necessity of studying it. I have seen that [this treatise] is found in many distinguished hands, even though I know that the rabbi did not edit it sufficiently, as he had intended to do. I was afraid that what happened to [his] commentary on the prayer of Rosh ha-Shanah would happen to it.¹ For it was stolen from me, and the one who printed it without my permission did not understand the intention of the author. Not only did he not correct what he found in his stolen [manuscript] from scribal errors, but he excised and diminished [the manuscript]. I hope, with God’s mercy and aid, to be able to correct this iniquity.

footnotes:
¹ It was published in Constantinople in 1576.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:5)


והואיל ואתא לידן נימא ביה מלתא כי אחרי שהרב אבא מרי ז"ל הסביר פנים שלא נאמר בברכת קדושת ה' של ימים נוראים רק ג' ובכן כדעת החכם החסיד אחי אמי הרשב"א הלוי זצ"ל הכריע אחר כך לומר ג"כ ובכן יתקדש וכו' כפי הנוסח המפורסם שכן מצא לו סמוכות במאמר מהזוהר וגם זה מן ההשמטות שראוי להגיה בפירוש התפלה הנז'.


Since [the opportunity] has come to our hands, I will say that after my teacher and father the rabbi, may his memory be a blessing, explained the reasons why we do not recite more than three “and therefores” in the blessings of the holiness of God during the Days of Awe,²). according to the opinion of my mother’s brother, the sage and pious rabbi Solomon ha-Levi Alkabetz,³ he subsequently decided to say as well, “And therefore may it be sanctified,” etc., according to the well-known version, for he found support [for this] in a section of the Zohar. This is also one of the omissions which should be inserted in the commentary.

footnotes:
² In the High Holiday liturgy, the three paragraphs after the Kedushah begin with the words “and therefore” (uvekhen
³ Moses Cordovero’s brother-in-law and teacher (ca. 1505-1584).


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:6)


וכאשר חשקה נפשי להתחיל בהגהת הקונטרס המעולה הזה ומצאתי את החכם השלם הרמ"ע מפאנו נר"ו בכל בית אבי נאמן הוא אשר אהב לכתוב לו רובי תורותיו בהם דבק מאד לאהבה וכבר קדם וזכה בו להגיהו ולהעמידו על מכונו מסכים בכל חלקיו עם מה שנתברר לו מכוונת הרב בשאר החבורים הנחמדים מזהב ומפז רב וכמשפט הזה עשה והצליח ג"כ לביאור **סדר עבודת יום הכפורים** אשר פירש הרב בדרכי הסוד


When my soul yearned to begin editing this distinguished treatise, I found the worthy sage, Rabbi Menaḥem Azariah of Fano,⁴ may the Merciful One guard and protect him, who was most faithful among all my father’s household. He [my father] loved to write to him most of his teachings, and he [Menaḥem Azariah] adhered to them lovingly. He has already proceeded [in this work] and succeeded in editing [these teachings] and placing them in their proper place in entire agreement with what was clear to him concerning the intentions of the master in the rest of [his] compositions, which are more valuable than gold. In this manner he has also succeeded in an explication of the liturgy of Yom Kippur which the master had explained according to the method of the secret [doctrine].⁵

footnotes:
⁴ Italian rabbi and Kabbalist (1548-1620). He was the foremost exponent of Moses Cordovero’s Kabbalistic system in Italy.
⁵ Published in Venice in 1587.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:7)


ואחזה אנכי אשית לבי לכל פרטי התקונים והנאוני קורא אני עליהם שפתים ישק משיב דברים נצוחים לכן שמח לבי ויגל כבודי והסכמתי לזכות בהם את הרבים והיה ראשיתנו מצער אולי יחנן ה' ואחריתנו ישגא מרד על דבר כבוד שמו ויהי נועם ה' אלדינו עלינו.


I saw [this] and directed my heart to [examine] all the detailed corrections, and they gave me joy. Concerning them I cited [the verse]: *He kisses the lips that give a right answer* (Prov. 24:26). *Therefore my heart is glad, and my glory rejoices* (Ps. 16:9). I have agreed to let the public benefit from them. *And though* our *beginning was small,* perhaps God will have mercy *and* our *end will greatly increase* (Job 8:7) for the sake of His glorious name. *May the graciousness of the Lord our God be upon us* (Ps. 90:17).


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/3:8)


אכי"ר:


Amen, so may His will be.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/4:1)


**הקדמת המגיה**


**EDITOR’S PREFACE**


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/4:2)


אמר המגיה


Said the editor:¹

footnotes:
¹ Presumably the Rabbi Israel referred to in Moses Bassola’s introduction.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/4:3)


מחנה אלקים זה דור דורשיו ישמח בעושיו כמחולת מחנים. ראו עינינו כן תמהו עולים מן הארץ לנטוע שמים וכצאת השמש בצהרים אור נגה עליהם. פתח דבריהם יאיר ויזהיר מחזה שדי להקדש למדי אל גנזי רזי תורה ערוכה ושמורה ממעונות אריות מהררי נמרים מעולפת ספירים


*This is God’s camp* (Gen. 32:3).² *The generation of those who seek Him* (Ps. 24:6) *will rejoice in His deeds* (Ps. 149:2) *like a dance of two companies* (Song of Songs 7:1). Our eyes have seen and were astonished [at these teachings] *arising out of the earth* (I Sam. 28:13) *to plant the heavens* (Isa. 51:16). Like the coming out of the sun at noontime, *a bright light is upon them* (Isa. 9:1). *The opening of* their *words gives light* (Ps. 119:130) and shines on a divine vision to those who are sanctified in the treasure-house of the secrets of Torah which is arrayed and guarded *from the lions’ dens, from the mountains of leopards* (Song 4:8) [and] *overlaid with sapphires* (Song 5:14).

footnotes:
² This preface, which is a pastiche of citations from the Bible and rabbinic literature, is exceedingly difficult to translate lucidly.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ohr%20Ne%27erav/s/Appendix%20The%20Introductory%20Material/r/4:4)


במסתרים מפני גוה כל היום דוה מזבח רשעים תועבה. ואף גם זאת מים רבים לא יוכלו לכבות את האהבה. כי ישרים דרכי ה'. ישפוט עניי עם ישפות שלום. וכי ידבר חילים יגבר. אל עמו ואל חסידיו.


*[My soul weeps] in secret places for your pride* (Jer. 13:17) *and is faint all the day* (Lam. 1:13). The altar *of the wicked is an abomination* (Prov. 21:27). Yet despite this, *many waters cannot quench love* (Song 8:7), *for upright are the ways of the Lord* (Hos. 14:10). *He will judge the poor of the people* (Ps. 72:4), *He will establish peace* (Isa. 26:12). When He speaks *He will overcome the mighty* (Eccles. 10:10) for His people and His pious ones.


###### Ohr Ne'erav, Appendix The Introductory Material 4:5

*Does not wisdom cry out* (Prov. 8:1) to the remnants of Israel *to observe discretion* (Prov. 2:11) [regarding] *the secrets of wisdom* (Job 11:6)? *They praise the Lord with their throats* (Ps. 149:6). In their right hand is *fullness of joy* (Ps. 16:11) and pleasantness. They direct their tongue *with the pen of a ready writer* (Ps. 45:2) who has studied and understood. He is frightened by the fools among the people. They walk in darkness, refined in the smelter *of poverty and much servitude* (Lam. 1:3) *bordered with cords* (Esther 1:6) of mockery, folly, and ignorance in *the captivity of this host* (Obad. 1:20) *when vileness is exalted among the sons of men* (Ps. 12:9). They are ignorant of the time to plant *the tree of life in the midst of the garden* (Gen. 2:9)

For from the quarrelsome words of the immature grain, a thorn has spread among the brethren *and rebellion like the sin of witchcraft* (I Sam. 15:23). *When the time comes she raises her wings on high* (Job 39:18). *God know the days of the perfect* (Ps. 37:18) *and the way of the righteous* (Ps. 1:6), and *raises up from the dust* (Ps. 113:7) and *the far distant sea* (Ps. 65:6) *all the souls of the house of Jacob* (Gen. 46:27). They all eagerly desire to hear the word of God *and the ancient records* (I Chron. 4:22). However, [until] now they have not seen the clear light of [Him who dwells] in the skies. *For from the top of the Rocks I see Him* (Num. 23:9), *and among those who turn the many to righteousness* (Dan. 12:3) *I behold Him. Grace is poured upon* their *lips* (Ps. 45:3) *like roses* (Song 5:13). Their waters are more sure than *the garden spring, a well of living waters* (Song 4:15). *Like doves [going] to their cotes* (Isa. 60:8) they have drunk spices. *They call to God, and He answers them* (Ps. 99:6). *This one says, I am the Lord’s* (Isa. 44:5), *answer me with broad spaces* (Ps. 118:5), for my *heart does not rest at night* (Eccles. 2;23), *nor does my spirit within me* (Isa. 26:9). *This one writes with his hand to the Lord* (Isa. 44:5) from that which had preceded him [at] Sinai, and on the *skins of kid goats* (Gen. 27:16) *they set up their signs* (Ps. 74:4) which they wrote to instruct them *for their generations, an eternal covenant* (Exod. 31:16).
